- **doubao-clinical-decision-support**: 循证医学临床辅助决策 Skill。用户需要分析临床问题，解释临床表现，提供循证医学判断时使用，用于针对临床问题，结合病例资料，查阅指南和文献等循证依据，进行科学回答与诊疗决策，进行诊断鉴别、检查路径、用药安全、治疗比较、预后和风险分析，或需要基于病例情况整理生成病历、入院记录、住院病程和会诊记录等文书。医学文献检索调研改用 doubao-medical-literature-search，单纯报告解读改用 doubao-medical-report，其他医学文献分析场景使用 doubao-medical 系列。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/doubao/skills/doubao-clinical-decision-support) · ★103)
- **doubao-clinical-decision-support**: 循证医学临床辅助决策 Skill。默认面向医疗从业者，围绕具体患者或临床问题，结合病例资料、指南与研究，分析诊断鉴别、检查路径、用药安全、治疗比较、预后和风险。普通用户主动选择本 Skill，或明确要求循证、查文献回答医疗问题时也可使用；患者自我问诊未要求循证或查文献时默认快速回答，并询问是否生成完整循证分析报告。病例丰富、问题复杂或需详细分析时生成完整报告。文献资料调研改用 doubao-medical-literature-search，单纯报告解读改用 doubao-medical-report；不用于文本处理或病历文书写作。 ([DeepJH/doubao-skill-and-info](https://github.com/DeepJH/doubao-skill-and-info/tree/HEAD/skills/doubao-clinical-decision-support) · ★7)

- **doubao-coding-design-solution**: 为现有代码库设计可实施、可验证、可迁移、可恢复的软件方案。适用于功能或架构方案、接口或数据变更、跨模块责任、可靠性机制、技术路线比较、迁移、灰度或回滚；不适用于直接编码、纯代码解读或主要依赖 benchmark/profile 的性能分析。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/doubao/skills/doubao-coding-design-solution) · ★103 · 2 copies)
- **doubao-coding-develop-backend-features**: 在既有代码库中实现已确认的新增服务端行为，或有意改变调用方可观察的既有行为，并交付可运行代码。已有故障或回归改用 Bugfix；关键契约或方案未决时先做设计；纯审查、性能专项、补测试或无行为变化的重构不使用。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/doubao/skills/doubao-coding-develop-backend-features) · ★103 · 3 copies)

- **doubao-coding-develop-unit-tests**: 当用户要求编写、补充、修复或运行单元测试，进行 TDD、为缺陷添加回归测试，或提升指定代码的单元测试覆盖率时使用。适用于函数、方法、类、组件、模块、包或代码变更；不用于端到端测试、完整集成测试、性能测试、仅做代码审查，或仅定位既有产品故障。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/doubao/skills/doubao-coding-develop-unit-tests) · ★103 · 3 copies)
- **doubao-coding-diagnose-and-fix-bugs**: 在已有代码出现报错、崩溃、错误结果、测试或构建失败、回归、环境差异或间歇故障时定位根因，并在用户要求修复时实施原因级补丁。新增需求或有意改变既有预期改用功能开发；正确预期尚未确定时先澄清或设计；纯审查、无故障重构、单纯补测试、无回归基线的性能优化或纯运维操作不使用。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/doubao/skills/doubao-coding-diagnose-and-fix-bugs) · ★103 · 3 copies)
- **doubao-coding-optimize-performance**: 当任务的裁决标准是可测量的性能指标（延迟、吞吐、CPU、内存、I/O、竞争、启动时间、扩展性）时使用：解释 profile/benchmark、建立可重复基线、定位瓶颈机制、验证单变量优化、建立性能回归守卫。用户问为什么慢、能否更快、内存为何增长、优化是否有效、如何 profile 或压测时触发。不用于普通功能调试、无测量的一般方案设计或代码审查；组合任务只处理性能子问题。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/doubao/skills/doubao-coding-optimize-performance) · ★103 · 3 copies)

- **doubao-contract-reviewer**: 专业审查合同并逐条批注。上传 .doc/.docx → 输出带 Track Changes + 批注的修订版 Word（.docx）。触发：合同审查/审核/审阅/看合同/帮我看看这份合同/合同风险/合同有没有问题/这份合同能签吗/合同把关/review contract/legal review；上传合同文件要求查问题也触发。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/doubao/skills/doubao-contract-reviewer) · ★103)
- **doubao-contract-reviewer**: doubao-contract-reviewer 是面向大众用户的合同审查 Skill，适合在豆包/豆包 Turbo 中审查各类合同。用户上传合同或询问“帮我审合同、合同有没有问题、这份合同能不能签、合同风险、合同把关、legal review”时必须使用。本 Skill 强制输出比裸跑更有用的结构化审查：先判断我方立场，再按交易模块识别风险，区分“必改风险 / 可争取优化项 / 形式完善项”，并给出可直接替换或补充的修改文本。适用于保密、买卖、服务、委托、借调、SaaS、许可、合作、租赁、渠道、数据处理等泛合同场景。 ([DeepJH/doubao-skill-and-info](https://github.com/DeepJH/doubao-skill-and-info/tree/HEAD/skills/doubao-contract-reviewer) · ★7 · 3 copies)

- **double-check**: Automatic final verification pass on completed deliverables. Runs at the end of every fable-mode run — those skills invoke it before delivery. Uses a panel of fresh checker agents when spawning is authorized (otherwise a cold self-check) to verify the finished output — not to redo the task. - **double-check**: Get a rigorous read-only second opinion on finished work through the host's available reviewer capabilities, preferably from a different model provider. Falls back to a fresh same-provider agent only when necessary and labels the reduced independence. Every review includes a mandatory scope-fidelity check against the original requirements — unrequested additions, unrequested removals, and deleted or weakened tests. Use when asked to double-check, verify, cross-check, or get a second opinion, and before shipping high-stakes or complex work. ([citypaul/.dotfiles](https://github.com/citypaul/.dotfiles/tree/HEAD/claude/.claude/skills/double-check) · ★760 · 2 copies)
- **double-check**: Verify a claim, assumption, or answer before acting on it or reporting it to the user. - **double-check**: WHEN finished work, review findings or a done claim needs a second opinion, or before shipping high-stakes work such as security, money, migrations or destructive changes; NOT for routine checks while building, unfinished drafts or a spec acceptance verdict; briefs a read-only reviewer cold, checks scope fidelity and closes each finding only on evidence.
